# Aeneas / Charon: GitHub issue and PR evidence (snapshot 2026-11-10)

All claims below cite GitHub issue or PR URLs. Each citation gives number, title, state and dates; "merged" means the PR was merged on that date. Source data was fetched on 2026-11-10 from the unauthenticated GitHub REST API. Raw JSON was saved to `notes/gh/` (not included in this repository; refetch with `scripts/fetchall.sh`), and the scripts that produced it are in `scripts/`.

## 0. Data coverage and gaps

| Dataset | AeneasVerif/aeneas | AeneasVerif/charon |
|---|---|---|
| Open issues | 237 | 69 |
| Open PRs | 109 | 5 |
| Issues closed 2025-11-10 .. 2026-11-10 | 116 | 204 |
| PRs closed/merged 2025-11-10 .. 2026-11-10 | 551 | 511 |
| GitHub releases (all `prerelease`, nightly/build) | 300 (2026-03-13 .. 2026-11-10) | 234 (2026-03-13 .. 2026-11-09) |
| Tags fetched | 200 of ~400 (pages 1-2) | 200 of ~300 (pages 1-2) |

How the data was collected and where it falls short:
- Closed items came from `state=closed&since=2025-11-01` (filtered by `updated_at`) and were then filtered locally to `closed_at >= 2025-11-10`. This catches every item closed in the window, because closing an item updates it. Older closed items fall outside the window.
- Comments were fetched for only 17 threads: aeneas #1501, #822, #1192, #958, #1053, #1043, #1206, #964, #464, #1519, #1193, #1199, #1410, #1112, #1227 and charon #955, #142 (which has no comments). For every other item, only the body was read.
- Neither repo has a stable or versioned GitHub release. All 534 releases are automated `nightly-*` or `build-*` prereleases, and their notes are auto-generated PR lists. The first release in both repos is dated 2026-03-13, so PRs merged before then (e.g. aeneas #698, #729, #779) do not appear in any release note. Charon issues mention Charon crate versions such as `0.1.301`, but these are not GitHub releases.
- The rate limit was never hit. About 45 of the 60 core requests were used, with 15 left.
- Topic tables (section 3) were built by regex over titles. For some topics the regex also ran over issue bodies; those rows are marked "(body match)". The tables are a triage aid and include false positives. The curated sections 1 and 2 are the evidence to rely on.

---

## 1. LOOPS AND ITERATORS: curated state of support

### 1.1 What has landed (merged PRs / fixed issues)

**Loop translation machinery (Aeneas)**
- [aeneas#655](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/655) "Do not join break contexts when there is a single break statement in a loop": merged 2025-11-26. [aeneas#1519](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1519) later says that #1519 is a regression from #655.
- [aeneas#689](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/689) "Improve `PrePasses.update_loops`": merged 2026-01-09. [aeneas#675](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/675) fixed an issue in the same prepass: merged 2025-12-14.
- [aeneas#732](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/732) "Fix many issues with the loop fixed point computations": merged 2026-02-07.
- [aeneas#779](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/779) "Use a `loop` fixed-point combinator when extracting loops": merged 2026-02-18.
  - Since this PR, loops are extracted to a `loop` combinator instead of recursive functions.
  - `-loops-to-rec` forces the old recursive translation.
  - Some loops that "dive" into recursive data structures (e.g. `while let List::Cons(..) = ls`) are still extracted as recursive functions. `-loops-no-rec` disables this.
- [aeneas#825](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/825) "Introduce auxiliary functions for loop bodies and reorder the loop inputs": merged 2026-03-09. It adds the `rust_loop` / `rust_loop_body` simp attributes and moves iterators to the first loop input.
- [aeneas#1219](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1219) "Add support for loops in consts": merged 2026-07-17. It fixes [aeneas#1140](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1140) "Loops within `const`/`static` trigger Internal error" (closed 2026-07-17).
- [aeneas#1348](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1348) "In `InterpLoopsFixedPoint.ml` join the break contexts from two iterations": merged 2026-09-16.

**Loop bugs fixed (closed issues)**
- [aeneas#964](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/964) "`Could not match context` error with conditional break in for loop": opened 2026-05-04, closed 2026-07-15. A commenter could not pin down the fix and suggested it was "perhaps #1095".
- [aeneas#1206](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1206) "`for` loop with conditional `break` and code after it: Could not match the contexts": opened 2026-07-13, closed 2026-09-16. A related repro in the comments (breaks with exits of different borrow shapes, `break None` vs `break Some(r)`) was posted 2026-08-15.
- [aeneas#1139](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1139) "`compute_loop_entry_fixed_point` hits `Unreachable`": opened 2026-06-19, closed 2026-07-16.
- [aeneas#789](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/789) "Assert_failure crash during loop context matching with tuple field access": closed 2026-03-16.
- [aeneas#1044](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1044) "Tuple field assignment with opaque function generates `()` ... in loop body": closed 2026-05-26.
- [aeneas#134](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/134) "Failure when extracting a loop with a shared borrow": opened 2024-04-16, closed 2026-02-04.
- [aeneas#351](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/351) "Bug in loop fixed-point computation": closed 2026-01-16.

**Control-flow reconstruction (Charon)**
- [charon#1051](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1051) "incorrect control-flow reconstruction in the presence of loops": opened 2026-03-12, closed 2026-06-26. In its repro, a `return` inside a loop was turned into a `break`. The fix is [charon#1306](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/pull/1306) "Fix loop misreconstruction" (merged 2026-06-26).
- [charon#899](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/899) "incorrect (not suboptimal) control-flow reconstruction": opened 2025-11-18, closed 2025-12-04. The repro was a range `for` loop in libcrux.

**Iterators (Aeneas Lean library models and extraction)**
- [aeneas#698](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/698) "Basic support for iterators": merged 2026-01-14. Adds the first iterator models to the Lean std library.
- [aeneas#711](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/711) "Start adding models for reasoning about array/vec iteration": merged 2026-01-19.
- [aeneas#715](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/715) "Add preliminary support for nested (mutable) borrows": merged 2026-01-28. The body says it is "not fully general yet but ... should be enough to support mutable iterators in loops", and that std iterator transformers would "come in a later PR".
- [aeneas#768](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/768) "Fix the model of core.iter.range.IteratorRange.next": merged 2026-02-13.
- [aeneas#786](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/786) "Add `enumerate` and `take` to `Iterators` and fix `loop.spec`": merged 2026-02-19.
- [aeneas#906](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/906) "Add support for mutable borrows inside shared borrows": merged 2026-04-04.
- [aeneas#1143](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1143) "Add models of iterator transformers": merged 2026-06-20. The PR body is empty.
- [aeneas#1220](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1220) "add test: for over vec of slices": merged 2026-07-22. It closes [aeneas#464](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/464) "Internal error when iterating on a Vec of slices" (opened 2025-03-24, closed 2026-07-22).
  - A maintainer comment on #464 (2026-07-17) lists the problems raised in that thread and their status:
    - `for` over a Vec: fixed.
    - `try_for_each` / `Try`: moved to #1222.
    - Missing Iterator `map` / `collect` fields: #1043 / PR #1131.
    - Nested borrows: "partly fixed (#181)", with #929 and #1186 still remaining.
    - Mutually recursive traits: #176.
- [aeneas#1131](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1131) "Fix `Iterator` extraction": merged 2026-09-03. It filters trait-impl fields down to those in the builtin model, which addresses [aeneas#1043](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1043). That issue was still **open** on 2026-11-10.
- [aeneas#1207](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1207) "returning `impl Iterator + '_` whose closure derefs a captured reference: Internal error": opened 2026-07-13, closed 2026-09-03. Related: [aeneas#1333](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1333) "Implement `PrePasses.fix_closure_signature_regions`", merged 2026-09-03.
- [aeneas#1309](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1309) "Add Lean models for `Step::{forward,backward}_overflowing`": merged 2026-08-27. Even so, [aeneas#1514](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1514) (open, 2026-10-30) reports that the `core.iter.range.Step` structure still lacks these fields, and [aeneas#1515](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1515) (open) adds them.
- Recent adapter models:
  - [aeneas#1386](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1386) "Model `next_back` for `slice::Iter`": merged 2026-10-28. Needed for `Rev`.
  - [aeneas#1373](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1373) "Model the `TakeWhile` iterator adapter": merged 2026-11-07.
  - [aeneas#1486](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1486) "Add a model of `IntoIterator for &mut [T; N]`": merged 2026-11-09.
  - [aeneas#1485](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1485) "Add a model of `core::array::from_fn`": merged 2026-11-04.
  - [aeneas#1380](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1380) (Option::map, bool::then, ...): merged 2026-10-08.

### 1.2 Open limitations: loop control flow

**Early returns, nested exits and labelled breaks**
- [aeneas#822](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/822) "Support for early returns inside loops that can be replaced with breaks": **open**, opened 2026-03-04.
  - The original repro now passes.
  - A comment from 2026-08-14 gives a new repro: an early `?` before a `for` loop with two `return`s inside it. It fails with `Early returns inside of loops are not supported yet` (PrePasses.ml).
  - A comment from 2026-09-26 confirms the failure still occurs on `nightly-2026.09.26` with a `while` loop over a slice.
- [aeneas#1501](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1501) "Breaks to outer loops are not supported yet for two returning loops in sequence": **open**, opened 2026-10-30.
  - Charon nests the second loop in the first loop's exit branch, so its `return` becomes `break 1`.
  - The error is `Breaks to outer loops are not supported yet` (PrePasses.ml:637).
  - A maintainer (Nadrieril) commented on 2026-10-30 that it would be nice if Charon recognised the two loops as sequential.
- [aeneas#1221](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1221) "Lean backend silently emits a bodyless axiom for a function with a return nested two loop-levels deep": **open**, opened 2026-07-18.
  - The issue calls the limitation "documented": returns inside nested loops and breaks to outer loops are not supported.
  - The complaint is that this case silently becomes an axiom instead of an error.
- [aeneas#958](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/958) "feat: Support nested loop exits": **closed unmerged** 2026-06-22. It was a ~6k-line AI-generated PR. The maintainer (sonmarcho) commented on 2026-06-22 that the agents "did many things wrong" and that he had "started working locally on several PRs to add support for nested loop exits ... incrementally."
- [aeneas#1506](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1506) "Treat calls to functions returning `!` as diverging": open.

**Join/fixpoint failures** ("Could not match the contexts", internal errors in InterpJoin / InterpMatchCtxs / InterpAbs / InterpReduceCollapse). All of these are **open**:
- [aeneas#930](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/930) "Could not match the contexts" (2026-04-13): a `for i in pts { return Err(()) }` followed by a match.
- [aeneas#1282](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1282) "Could not match the contexts on a loop" (2026-08-21): a `for` over a Vec of opaque `RwLock`s with a recursive call.
- [aeneas#1349](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1349) "error when matching contexts" (2026-09-16).
- [aeneas#1399](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1399) "Internal error in `InterpMatchCtxs` when joining loop contexts over non-opaque wrapper holding borrow" (2026-10-05).
- [aeneas#1368](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1368) "internal error at `InterpMatchCtxs.ml:230` when joining contexts that hold a struct of borrowed slices" (2026-09-26).
- [aeneas#1369](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1369) "internal error at `InterpReduceCollapse.ml:1137` on `if let Some(x) = slice.get(i)` inside a loop" (2026-09-26).
- [aeneas#1319](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1319) "Internal error (Interp.ml:609) on `<[T]>::get` inside a loop" (2026-08-28).
- [aeneas#1513](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1513) "Unimplemented when a loop reads an array after an indexed write to it" (2026-10-30). The failing case is the `VSharedLoan, VMutLoan` TODO in InterpMatchCtxs.ml:420, and the Lean output is `sorry`.
- [aeneas#1519](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1519) "Early break causes problems when joining abstractions" (2026-11-02). A comment says it is a regression from #655.
- [aeneas#1459](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1459) "Internal error in `simplify_loop_output_conts` when code after a loop reads one field of a tuple loop variable" (2026-10-16).
- [aeneas#1162](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1162) "Internal error on a loop inside a trait method" (2026-06-24): a `while let Some(x) = self.next()`.
- [aeneas#1395](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1395) "fieldless enum variant written into an array is translated as `()` when a later loop borrows the array" (2026-10-03). This is a miscompilation, not a crash.
- [aeneas#1223](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1223) "join of loan values at a control-flow merge is not sufficiently precise" (2026-07-20).
- [aeneas#1518](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1518) "`unit_vars_to_unit` makes backwards fn arguments `PIgnored`, producing crashes" (2026-11-02). The repro uses a loop.

**Recursion through loops**
- [aeneas#1367](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1367) "no `partial_fixpoint` monotonicity lemma for `loop`, so recursion through a loop does not elaborate": **open**, 2026-09-26.
  - Aeneas reports no errors, but Lean rejects the output with "Could not prove ... to be monotone".
  - The reporter supplies the missing lemma in the issue.

**Charon control-flow stability**
- [charon#955](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/955) "more stable looping control flow": **open**, 2025-12-22. Code after a loop sometimes ends up inside the `None` arm, which breaks loop resugaring (reported for Eurydice). A maintainer replied on 2026-01-05: "definitely something we'd like to fix".
- [charon#523](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/523) "Suboptimal CFG reconstruction": **open**, 2025-01-14.
- [charon#803](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/803) "duplication of basic-blocks for test conditions of the form e1 && e2": **open**, 2025-08-13.

### 1.3 Open limitations: iterators, adapters and closures used with iterators

**Crashes or Unimplemented during translation**
- [aeneas#1222](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1222) "`Iterator::try_for_each` causes Aeneas to abort with Internal error": **open**, 2026-07-20.
  - Fails at `SymbolicToPureTypes.ml:1012` (translate_fun_sigs) with an uncaught `CFailure`.
  - The issue says `xs.iter().map(|&x| x + 1).collect()` and `xs.iter().try_for_each(...)` produce the same error.
- [aeneas#1192](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1192) "second loop zipping two mutable slice iterators": **open**, 2026-07-07.
  - `for _ in inputs.iter_mut().zip(scratch.iter_mut()) {}` fails with an internal error at InterpBorrowsCore.ml:1498. The pattern comes from curve25519-dalek.
  - [aeneas#1314](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1314) "Fix translation of iterators over arrays" (open, WIP) targets it.
  - A comment from 2026-10-07 links an outside fork with a fix (no PR).
- [aeneas#1053](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1053) "`Iterator::zip` test check fails with `RErased`": **open**, 2026-05-22.
  - A maintainer said on 2026-05-25 that he was working on a fix.
  - The reporter said on 2026-05-27 that `for _ in a.iter().zip(b.iter()) {}` still fails.
- [aeneas#1193](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1193) "trait method of a reference-parameterized trait passed to `.map` is Unimplemented": **open**, 2026-07-08.
  - `xs.iter().map(S::from)` fails. The workaround is a closure: `.map(|x| S::from(x))`.
  - A comment from 2026-09-07 lists more failing cases (RegionsHierarchy.ml:190).
- [aeneas#1435](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1435) "Uncaught exception: `CFailure`": **open**, 2026-10-13. Triggered by `bytes.iter().copied()` passed to a generic `impl Iterator` function.
- [aeneas#1343](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1343) "type-decl holding a `Box<dyn Iterator>` field is refused with Dynamic trait types are not supported yet": **open**, 2026-09-10.

**Extraction succeeds but the Lean output is ill-typed or incomplete**
- [aeneas#1437](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1437) "Missing backwards function in Lean backend with iterators": **open**, 2026-10-14. With `impl Iterator` taken by `&mut`, `Iterator.next` gets type `I → Result (Option _ × I × (I → I))` where `... × I` is expected.
- [aeneas#1152](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1152) "Lean backend can generate Iterator.next with extra back-function component": **open**, 2026-06-22.
- [aeneas#1153](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1153) "Lean backend can generate IntoIter.zip call with unsolved implicit allocator parameter": **open**, 2026-06-22.
- [aeneas#1043](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1043) "Iterator trait impl generates `map :=` and `collect :=` fields": **open**, 2026-05-20. Seen with `.enumerate().map(...).collect()`. The fix PR #1131 was merged 2026-09-03, but the issue was not closed.

**Adapters with no model (emitted as axioms) or a pending model**
- [aeneas#1511](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1511) "no model for `Iterator::chain`": **open**, 2026-10-30. `Chain`, `chain` and `next` are emitted as axioms.
- [aeneas#1510](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1510) "Model `Iterator::map` and `Iterator::chain`": **open PR**, 2026-10-30. Per its body, ".map(f) and .chain(other) translated to axioms" before this PR.
- [aeneas#1542](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1542) "Model Iterator::fold, DoubleEndedIterator::rfold, and Rev::fold": **open PR**, 2026-11-07. Per its body, "`Iterator::fold` has no Lean model".
- Other open model PRs:
  - [aeneas#1546](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1546) TakeWhile::fold.
  - [aeneas#1544](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1544) slice::Iter::any.
  - [aeneas#1552](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1552) Iterator::count.
  - [aeneas#1057](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1057) DoubleEndedIterator/Rev, open since 2026-05-25.

**Wrong models (soundness-relevant)**
- [aeneas#1408](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1408) "signed `Step::backward_checked` model uses the unsigned bound, so `rev()` over a signed range panics": open, 2026-10-09.
- [aeneas#1287](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1287) "`IScalarStep.backward_checked` is falsely modeled as panicking when going negative": open, 2026-08-24.
- [aeneas#1534](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1534) "`RangeFrom::get_mut` model writes changes back to the wrong part of the slice": open, 2026-11-03.
- [aeneas#1514](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1514) "`core.iter.range.Step` structure lacks `forward_overflowing` / `backward_overflowing`": open, 2026-10-30. Triggered by a plain `for i in 0..4`.

**Proof support for loops and iterators**
- [aeneas#1132](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1132) "core::iter::range::IteratorRange::next has no reasoning lemma, range-for loops can't be proven": **open**, 2026-06-17. Quote: "Aeneas can extract range loops but can't ergonomically prove them."
- Open PRs for this gap:
  - [aeneas#1133](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1133) "range iterator next step spec" (2026-06-17).
  - [aeneas#1468](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1468) "Add total step specifications for usize range iteration" (2026-10-19, "Closes #1132").
  - [aeneas#992](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/992) "collapse slice iterator loops to List.foldl" (2026-05-07).
  - [aeneas#599](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/599) "Generalize the `Result` type and implement a loop invariant inferencer" (open since 2025-08-22).
- [aeneas#1107](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1107) "Remove the custom `Range` definitions": open, 2026-06-09.

**Closures (as used with iterators).** All of these are **open**:
- [aeneas#1266](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1266) "Closure returning reference argument fails with non-endable abstraction" (2026-08-15). Reduced from `Option::and_then(|v| v.as_ref())`.
- [aeneas#1119](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1119) "Internal error translating a function that returns a closure which borrows its environment (`-> impl Fn + 'a`)" (2026-06-11).
- [aeneas#1046](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1046) "FnOnce problem: `call_once` return type is `Result (closure state)` instead of `Result Unit`" (2026-05-20).
- [aeneas#960](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/960) "Wrong binder for call to `FnMut` closure returning unit" (2026-04-28). The generated Lean does not type-check.
- [aeneas#924](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/924) "Extraction fails for closure with if-then-else and variable capture" (2026-04-09).
- [aeneas#804](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/804) "Error with reference produced by a closure" (2026-02-25).
- [aeneas#1165](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1165) "Forward reference with closure in default method body" (2026-06-24).
- [aeneas#1503](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1503) "Support ADTs containing nested mutable borrows" (2026-10-30). Quote: "The common case is a closure capturing a `&mut`-holding struct by mutable reference."
- [charon#1040](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1040) "missing lifetime information about upvars": open, labels `needed-for-aeneas` and `S-blocked-on-rustc`.

**Closure items fixed**
- [aeneas#86](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/86) "Add support for closures" (tracking issue): closed 2026-02-04.
- [aeneas#891](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/891) "Error with closure taking a reference": closed 2026-07-09.
- [aeneas#1141](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1141) "Function taking `impl Fn(&T)` triggers Internal error": closed 2026-07-02.
- [aeneas#718](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/718) "Fix minor issues and add closure translation tests": merged 2026-01-29.
- [aeneas#762](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/762) "Add basic support for using top-level functions as function pointers": merged 2026-02-12.

### 1.4 Overall state of loops and iterators

**Loops**
- Simple loops translate: `while`, `loop`, and `for` over ranges, slices and Vecs (including Vec of slices, since #1220).
- Since February 2026, loops are extracted with a `loop` fixed-point combinator (#779), with auxiliary body definitions (#825).
- **Not supported (reported as "not supported yet"):**
  - early `return` inside loops in many shapes (#822);
  - breaks/returns to outer loops in nested or sequential loops (#1501, #1221). The one attempt at a general fix, #958, was rejected, and incremental work was announced on 2026-06-22.
- The most common failure class is "Could not match the contexts" and related internal errors when joining loop contexts that hold borrows (§1.2). Several were fixed (#964, #1206, #1139) and at least 13 remained open as of 2026-11-10.
- **Soundness-adjacent risks:**
  - an unsupported loop shape can degrade silently to an axiom (#1221);
  - a loop can be miscompiled (#1395).

**Iterators**
- There is a growing hand-written Lean model library: Range, slice/array/Vec iteration, enumerate, take, zip, rev/next_back, TakeWhile, `&mut [T; N]`.
- **Pending or missing models:** `map`, `chain`, `fold`, `count`, `any`. These are emitted as axioms, and models exist only in open PRs as of 2026-11-10.
- **Translation-blocking:**
  - `try_for_each`, plus the `map().collect()` signature crash (#1222);
  - `zip` over two `iter_mut()`s (#1192);
  - `zip` with RErased (#1053);
  - `.map(Trait::method)` (#1193);
  - `.copied()` into generic `impl Iterator` (#1435);
  - `Box<dyn Iterator>` (#1343).
- **Ill-typed Lean output in generic-iterator settings:** #1437, #1152, #1153, #1043.
- **Proving range-`for` loops** still lacks a library `next` spec (#1132). PRs #1133 and #1468 were open.

---

## 2. Other limitation themes (curated highlights)

**`dyn Trait`**
- Supported so far:
  - [aeneas#729](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/729) "Add preliminary support for `dyn` trait objects": merged 2026-02-05, "mostly to enable the extraction of `Debug` instances".
  - On the Charon side, `dyn Trait` is checked off in [charon#142](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/142).
  - [charon#1042](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/pull/1042) "Support `dyn Trait` with `--monomorphize`": merged 2026-03-24. The feature request [charon#856](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/856) was still open.
- Still unsupported:
  - [aeneas#1500](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1500) "Method calls through `&dyn Trait` are not supported (Function pointers are not supported yet)": open, 2026-10-30.
  - [aeneas#1507](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1507) "Allow identity upcasts of `dyn` values containing borrows": open.
  - [aeneas#1343](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1343): `Box<dyn Iterator>` field.
  - [aeneas#1243](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1243): `sorry`-named structure emitted for the dyn-compatible trait `digest::DynDigest`. Open, 2026-07-31.
- Charon dyn/vtable fixes (all closed in the window):
  - [charon#1285](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1285) supertrait wrong vtable: closed 2026-08-26.
  - [charon#1388](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1388) closure Fn* vtables never emitted: closed 2026-09-11.
  - [charon#1390](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1390) drops not desugared for dyn: closed 2026-08-31.

**Unsafe, raw pointers, unions, FFI and inline asm**
- [aeneas#743](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/743) "Deny unsafe *ops* but not unsafe *blocks*": closed 2026-02-10. Its body notes that Aeneas "doesn't support unsafe code because it can't model the semantics".
- [aeneas#1003](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1003) "Improve the error message that occurs when dereferencing raw pointers": merged 2026-05-08.
- Heap model for raw pointers:
  - [aeneas#1428](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1428): merged 2026-10-13.
  - Follow-ups still open: [aeneas#1548](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1548), [aeneas#1453](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1453) (MaybeUninit / raw-pointer models), [aeneas#1454](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1454) (NonNull), [aeneas#1469](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1469), [aeneas#1537](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1537), [aeneas#976](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/976).
  - [aeneas#1433](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1433) was closed unmerged.
- Unions: [aeneas#1199](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1199) "Union support": open, 2026-07-10.
  - Aeneas rejects unions in TypesAnalysis.ml with "unions are not supported".
  - A maintainer suggested modelling non-punning unions as enums.
  - Nadrieril (2026-07-10): "Aeneas today does not have any models related to layouts and byte representations."
- Inline asm: [aeneas#1396](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1396) "Translate inline asm using Kraken": open.
- zerocopy panics: [aeneas#912](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/912) and [aeneas#913](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/913), both open.
- Charon: [charon#1449](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1449) "annotate unsafe operations" closed 2026-10-01. Unsafe code is checked off in charon#142.

**Interior mutability, Drop and atomics**
- [aeneas#1525](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1525) "Cell::set / Atomic*::fetch_add through &self are modelled as pure ...": open, 2026-11-03. Proofs can be false.
- [aeneas#974](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/974) "Add AtomicU8 and AtomicU64 as builtin external types": open PR.
- [aeneas#1410](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1410) "user `Drop` impls never run, so a destructor's effect is silently lost": open, 2026-10-09.
  - Nadrieril (2026-10-28) acknowledged it: "something we're aware of and should have documented properly; ... even warning is a hard problem".
  - A comment on the same issue reports that a panicking destructor yields a total (non-panicking) extraction.
- No issues in the window were specifically about Rc/RefCell/Mutex beyond the above.

**async / coroutines**
- [aeneas#1265](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1265) "Feature request: `async`": open. It is blocked on [charon#609](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/609) "Support async" (open since 2025-03-14, unchecked in charon#142).
- [charon#1602](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/pull/1602) "Translate coroutines": open PR, 2026-11-09. Its body describes it as a "First step towards #609". Async closures are not supported yet.

**Traits**
- GATs: [aeneas#1112](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1112) "Add support for GATs": open. Nadrieril (2026-07-31): this "seems to fit squarely under 'Aeneas does not support GATs'".
  - Charon warns that GATs cannot work with `--lift-associated-types`, which `--preset=aeneas` implies.
  - [aeneas#1195](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1195): hard crash with certain GATs. Open.
- HRTB: [aeneas#1158](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1158) "Add preliminary support for higher ranked trait bounds": merged 2026-07-02.
  - [aeneas#1227](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1227) "Full support for HRTB": open. It depends on [charon#1143](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1143), which closed 2026-10-15.
  - [charon#1362](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1362): associated type of a `for<'a>` bound becomes `type_error`. Open.
- Mutual recursion:
  - [aeneas#176](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/176) "mutually recursive trait declarations": open since 2024. [aeneas#703](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/703) (merged 2026-01-19) downgraded this to a warning.
  - [aeneas#1264](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1264) "mutually recursive functions and trait implementations": open. Example: recursion via `FnOnce` gives "Detected groups of mixed mutually recursive definitions".
  - [aeneas#1494](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1494) "nonpos_structure for recursive traits": open.
- Also open:
  - [aeneas#1493](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1493): cyclic associated-type constraint.
  - [aeneas#1492](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1492): could not resolve recursive fields.
  - [charon#1548](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1548): panic with specialisation.
  - [charon#1078](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1078): self-referential clause in default associated type.

**Borrows and lifetimes**
- Nested borrows:
  - [aeneas#715](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/715) (preliminary, merged 2026-01-28) and [aeneas#906](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/906) (merged 2026-04-04).
  - [aeneas#181](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/181) "nested borrows in function signatures": closed 2026-06-30.
  - Still open: [aeneas#929](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/929) "Unsupported nested borrow", [aeneas#1186](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1186) (array of slice refs in a loop), [aeneas#1503](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1503) (ADTs with nested `&mut`), [aeneas#1502](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1502) (`&mut &mut &mut T`), [aeneas#1215](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1215) and [aeneas#1214](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1214) (generics instantiated with `&mut`).
- `'static`: [aeneas#1438](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1438) "Add support for the 'static region": merged 2026-10-15. Regressions were fixed in [aeneas#1472](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1472). [aeneas#727](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/727) "Add support for `'static`" was still open.
- Charon: [charon#1237](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1237) "Precisely track late-bound lifetimes": open, `needed-for-aeneas`.

**Scalars, floats and strings**
- Floats: [aeneas#828](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/828) "Error: unsupported floats": open, 2026-03-11. [aeneas#1073](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1073) "Initial draft of basic float support for lean backend": open.
- Checked binops: [aeneas#1257](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1257) "AddChecked/SubChecked/MulChecked binops abort translation": open. [aeneas#1258](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1258) is an open PR for it.
- `u8 as char`: [aeneas#1251](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1251), open.
- Strings:
  - [aeneas#697](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/697) "add support for strings": merged 2026-01-12. [aeneas#117](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/117) "Add String to the Lean library": closed 2026-07-08.
  - Still open: [aeneas#838](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/838) (failure on string functions), [aeneas#1178](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1178) (C-string literals), [aeneas#797](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/797) (matching on chars throws an uncaught exception).
- Model bugs in other backends: [aeneas#1521](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1521) (F*/Coq/HOL4 signed division semantics) and [aeneas#1524](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1524) (F*/Coq casts), both closed 2026-11-03.

**Soundness of models and axioms (Lean)**
- [aeneas#1526](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1526): `array::repeat` doesn't call `clone`.
- [aeneas#1409](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1409): `contains` argument order.
- [aeneas#1275](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1275): `IScalar.rem` MIN % -1.
- [aeneas#1288](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1288): `vec::insert` model wrong.
- [aeneas#1116](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1116): Rocq `mk_array` unsound.
- [aeneas#1516](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/1516): `import Aeneas` adds 15 axioms.
- All of these were open as of 2026-11-10.

**Charon's own tracking issue** [charon#142](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/142) "Tracking issue for unsupported rust features" (open):
- **Checked off:** dyn Trait, `for<'a>` bounds, string constants, enum-as-isize casts, type aliases, associated-type equalities, mutually recursive traits, defaulted methods, Drop semantics, `vec!`, unsafe code, GATs, RPITIT, thread-locals, TypeId, pattern types.
- **Unchecked:** `async` (#609), `CoerceUnsized` (#855), contract annotations (#646), default field values, unsafe fields.

## 3. Release notes (both repos)

- **Both repos**
  - Releases are automated `nightly-YYYY.MM.DD[-sha]` and earlier `build-<timestamp>-<sha>` prereleases.
  - The first release is dated 2026-03-13 (aeneas: `build-2026.03.13.164108-bd6a7ee...`; charon: `build-2026.03.13.163309-923e13b...`). This matches [aeneas#834](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/issues/834) / [charon#1053](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/charon/issues/1053) "Publish pre-builts on GitHub", closed 2026-03-13.
  - There are no semantic-version releases. The only non-nightly tag is `icfp2022`.
- **Aeneas**
  - Latest: `nightly-2026.11.10-0d21a9e` (2026-11-10).
  - 300 releases.
  - Lean toolchain at the latest release: v4.36.0-rc2, per [aeneas#1547](https://github.com/AeneasVerif/aeneas/pull/1547).
- **Charon**
  - Latest: `nightly-2026.11.09` (2026-11-09).
  - 234 releases.
